8px rgba(16,185,129,.3)}.st11g-btn.check:hover{background:#059669}.st11g-btn.reset{background:#64748b;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(100,116,139,.3)}.st11g-btn.reset:hover{background:#475569}.st11g-fb{display:none;margin-top:12px;padding:14px;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.6}.st11g-fb.show{display:block;animation:fadeIn2 .3s}.st11g-fb.correct{background:#dcfce7;color:#15803d;border-left:4px solid #22c55e}.st11g-fb.incorrect{background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c;border-left:4px solid #ef4444}.st11g-score{display:flex;gap:12px;margin-top:10px;flex-wrap:wrap}.st11g-score-item{background:#fff;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0}@keyframes fadeIn2{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}@media(max-width:600px){.st11g-input{width:140px}.st11g-box{padding:15px}}</style><div class="st11g-wrap"><div class="st11g-box"><h2 class="st11g-title">📝 Grammar Practice: Present Simple vs Present Continuous</h2><div class="st11g-section" id="sec-st11g-part1"><h3>🟢 Part 1: Present Simple (Habits & Facts)</h3><div class="st11g-instruction"><b>Задание:</b> Раскрой скобки, поставив глагол в <b>Present Simple</b>. Обращай внимание на подлежащее (ед./мн. число, лицо) и наречия частотности (<b>always, usually, often, sometimes, never</b>). В 3-м лице ед. числа не забудь окончание <b>-s/-es</b>!</div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="1"><span class="st11g-q-num">1</span><span class="st11g-q-text">My sister always <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">help</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q1" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> me with my homework. She is very kind.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="1">💡 <b>helps</b> — подлежащее <b>my sister</b> = <b>she</b> (3-е лицо ед. числа) + наречие <b>always</b> → Present Simple с окончанием <b>-s</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="2"><span class="st11g-q-num">2</span><span class="st11g-q-text">I never <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">talk</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q2" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> in class. I am very polite.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="2">💡 <b>talk</b> — подлежащее <b>I</b> + наречие <b>never</b> → Present Simple без окончания (только 3-е лицо ед. числа получает <b>-s</b>).</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="3"><span class="st11g-q-num">3</span><span class="st11g-q-text">My brother often <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">play</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q3" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> computer games after school.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="3">💡 <b>plays</b> — подлежащее <b>my brother</b> = <b>he</b> (3-е лицо ед. числа) + наречие <b>often</b> → <b>plays</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="4"><span class="st11g-q-num">4</span><span class="st11g-q-text">We usually <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">watch</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q4" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> TV in the evening with our parents.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="4">💡 <b>watch</b> — подлежащее <b>we</b> + наречие <b>usually</b> → Present Simple без окончания.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="5"><span class="st11g-q-num">5</span><span class="st11g-q-text">She sometimes <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">read</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q5" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> comics before bed.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="5">💡 <b>reads</b> — подлежащее <b>she</b> (3-е лицо ед. числа) + наречие <b>sometimes</b> → <b>reads</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="6"><span class="st11g-q-num">6</span><span class="st11g-q-text">My parents always <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">listen</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q6" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> to music in the car.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="6">💡 <b>listen</b> — подлежащее <b>my parents</b> = <b>they</b> (мн. число) → Present Simple без окончания.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="7"><span class="st11g-q-num">7</span><span class="st11g-q-text">He never <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">collect</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q7" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> stamps. He collects coins.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="7">💡 <b>collects</b> — подлежащее <b>he</b> (3-е лицо ед. числа) + наречие <b>never</b> → <b>collects</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="8"><span class="st11g-q-num">8</span><span class="st11g-q-text">I often <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">go</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q8" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> to the cinema with my friends.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="8">💡 <b>go</b> — подлежащее <b>I</b> + наречие <b>often</b> → Present Simple без окончания.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="9"><span class="st11g-q-num">9</span><span class="st11g-q-text">My cousin sometimes <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">play</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q9" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> chess with his grandfather.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="9">💡 <b>plays</b> — подлежащее <b>my cousin</b> = <b>he</b> (3-е лицо ед. числа) + наречие <b>sometimes</b> → <b>plays</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="10"><span class="st11g-q-num">10</span><span class="st11g-q-text">They usually <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">spend</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q10" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> holidays with their grandparents.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="10">💡 <b>spend</b> — подлежащее <b>they</b> (мн. число) + наречие <b>usually</b> → Present Simple без окончания.</div></div><button class="st11g-btn check" onclick="checkSt11gPart1()">✓ Check Part 1</button><button class="st11g-btn reset" onclick="resetSt11gPart1()">↺ Reset</button><div id="st11g-fb-part1" class="st11g-fb"></div></div><div class="st11g-section" id="sec-st11g-part2"><h3>🔵 Part 2: Present Continuous (Actions Now)</h3><div class="st11g-instruction"><b>Задание:</b> Раскрой скобки, поставив глагол в <b>Present Continuous</b> (<b>am/is/are + V-ing</b>). Обращай внимание на маркеры: <b>now, at the moment, right now, Look!</b> — они указывают на действие в момент речи.</div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="11"><span class="st11g-q-num">1</span><span class="st11g-q-text">Look! My sister <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">help</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q11" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> her friend with her homework now.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="11">💡 <b>is helping</b> — маркер <b>Look!</b> и <b>now</b> → Present Continuous. Подлежащее <b>my sister</b> = <b>she</b> → <b>is helping</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="12"><span class="st11g-q-num">2</span><span class="st11g-q-text">I <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">read</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q12" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> a comic book at the moment.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="12">💡 <b>am reading</b> — маркер <b>at the moment</b> → Present Continuous. С <b>I</b> → <b>am reading</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="13"><span class="st11g-q-num">3</span><span class="st11g-q-text">My brother <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">play</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q13" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> computer games right now.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="13">💡 <b>is playing</b> — маркер <b>right now</b> → Present Continuous. Подлежащее <b>my brother</b> = <b>he</b> → <b>is playing</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="14"><span class="st11g-q-num">4</span><span class="st11g-q-text">We <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">watch</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q14" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> TV right now with our parents.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="14">💡 <b>are watching</b> — маркер <b>right now</b> → Present Continuous. С <b>we</b> → <b>are watching</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="15"><span class="st11g-q-num">5</span><span class="st11g-q-text">She <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">listen</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q15" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> to music now.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="15">💡 <b>is listening</b> — маркер <b>now</b> → Present Continuous. Подлежащее <b>she</b> → <b>is listening</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="16"><span class="st11g-q-num">6</span><span class="st11g-q-text">They <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">play</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q16" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> chess in the garden at the moment.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="16">💡 <b>are playing</b> — маркер <b>at the moment</b> → Present Continuous. С <b>they</b> → <b>are playing</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="17"><span class="st11g-q-num">7</span><span class="st11g-q-text">My parents <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">talk</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q17" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> to my teacher now.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="17">💡 <b>are talking</b> — маркер <b>now</b> → Present Continuous. Подлежащее <b>my parents</b> = <b>they</b> → <b>are talking</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="18"><span class="st11g-q-num">8</span><span class="st11g-q-text">He <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">collect</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q18" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> stamps at the moment.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="18">💡 <b>is collecting</b> — маркер <b>at the moment</b> → Present Continuous. Подлежащее <b>he</b> → <b>is collecting</b>.</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="19"><span class="st11g-q-num">9</span><span class="st11g-q-text">I <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">not watch</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q19" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> TV now. I am doing my homework.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="19">💡 <b>am not watching</b> — маркер <b>now</b> → Present Continuous в отрицании. С <b>I</b> → <b>am not watching</b> (или <b>'m not watching</b>).</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="20"><span class="st11g-q-num">10</span><span class="st11g-q-text">Look! The twins <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">play</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q20" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> in the garden.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="20">💡 <b>are playing</b> — маркер <b>Look!</b> → Present Continuous. Подлежащее <b>the twins</b> = <b>they</b> → <b>are playing</b>.</div></div><button class="st11g-btn check" onclick="checkSt11gPart2()">✓ Check Part 2</button><button class="st11g-btn reset" onclick="resetSt11gPart2()">↺ Reset</button><div id="st11g-fb-part2" class="st11g-fb"></div></div><div class="st11g-section" id="sec-st11g-part3"><h3>🟣 Part 3: Mixed Practice (Present Simple vs Present Continuous)</h3><div class="st11g-instruction"><b>Задание:</b> Выбери правильное время — <b>Present Simple</b> (привычки, факты, наречия частотности) или <b>Present Continuous</b> (действие сейчас). Раскрой скобки в обоих случаях, если нужно. Обращай внимание на маркеры!</div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="21"><span class="st11g-q-num">1</span><span class="st11g-q-text">I usually <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">play</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q21" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> chess, but today I <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">play</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q21b" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> computer games.</span><div class="st11g-expl" data-expl="21">💡 <b>play</b> (обычно — Present Simple с <b>usually</b>) / <b>am playing</b> (сегодня — Present Continuous, действие сейчас).</div></div><div class="st11g-q" data-q="22"><span class="st11g-q-num">2</span><span class="st11g-q-text">My sister always <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">help</span><input type="text" class="st11g-input" id="st11g-q22" placeholder="" oninput="st11gHideHint(this);st11gAutoSave()"></span> me, but now she <span class="st11g-input-wrap"><span class="st11g-input-hint">not Используй лексику темы 1.1.</div><div class="wr-requirements"><h4>📋 Что нужно включить в текст:</h4><ul class="wr-req-list"><li>Имя друга/подруги и возраст</li><li>Описание волос (curly / straight / freckles)</li><li>Минимум 3 прилагательных характера (kind, funny, clever, shy, talkative, polite, hard-working и т.д.)</li><li>Что друг/подруга делает сейчас (Present Continuous: is reading, is got <select><option>___</option><option>curly</option><option>straight</option><option>wavy</option></select> <select><option>___</option><option>blonde</option><option>dark</option><option>brown</option><option>red</option></select> hair and <select><option>___</option><option>blue</option><option>green</option><option>brown</option></select> eyes. <select><option>___</option><option>She</option><option>He</option></select> is <select><option>___</option><option>tall</option><option>short</option></select> and <select><option>___</option><option>slim</option><option>plump</option></select>. <select><option>___</option><option>She has</option><option>He has</option><option>She hasn't got</option><option>He hasn't got</option></select> <select><option>___</option><option>freckles</option><option>glasses</option></select> on <select><option>___</option><option>her</option><option>his</option></select> face. <select><option>___</option><option>She</option><option>He</option></select> is very <select><option>___</option><option>kind</option><option>funny</option><option>clever</option><option>friendly</option></select>, <select><option>___</option><option>talkative</option><option>shy</option><option>hard-working</option><option>polite</option></select> and <select><option>___</option><option>generous</option><option>lazy</option><option>mean</option><option>rude</option></select>. Right now <select><option>___</option><option>she</option><option>he</option></select> <select><option>___</option><option>is reading</option><option>is playing</option><option>is drawing</option><option>is listening to music</option></select>. <select><option>___</option><option>She</option><option>He</option></select> usually <select><option>___</option><option>plays</option><option>reads</option><option>studies</option><option>draws</option></select> after school. I love <select><option>___</option><option>her</option><option>him</option></select> because <select><option>___</option><option>we are great friends</option><option>she is always kind to me</option><option>he makes me laugh</option></select>!</div><p style="margin-top:12px;color:#64748b;font-size:13px">💡 <b>Совет:</b> Можешь менять варианты или добавлять свои собственные слова! Не забудь использовать <b>объектные местоимения</b> (her/him) и <b>притяжательные</b> (her/his).</p></div></details></div></div>
